# outputs/03-strategy-designer.md
title: "Strategy Designer: Framework for AI System Alignment Evaluation"
date: 2025-09-02
status: draft
---

## 1. Introduction
The objective of this document is to outline a comprehensive strategy for d[1D[K
designing, evaluating, and implementing AI system alignment metrics. This f[1D[K
framework is intended for use by financial analysts and traders to ensure r[1D[K
robust and safe AI-driven strategies.

## 2. Methodology Overview
The strategy designer will leverage the findings from the previous phases t[1D[K
to develop a robust evaluation framework. The framework will include a mult[4D[K
multi-metric scoring system with dynamic adversarial testing and will be ap[2D[K
applied to a specific trading market, guided by the `SYMBOL_SPREAD` paramet[7D[K
parameter.

## 3. Evaluation Framework Development
The evaluation framework will be composed of several key components:
1. **Multi-Metric Scoring System:** Combines `Truthfulness`, `Helpfulness`,[14D[K
`Helpfulness`, `Harmlessness`, and `Robustness` metrics.
2. **Dynamic Adversarial Testing:** Rotates through a suite of adversarial [K
prompts and attack vectors to ensure robustness and safety.
3. **Human In-the-Loop (HITL):** Uses a calibrated rubric for `Helpfulness`[13D[K
`Helpfulness` and `Harmlessness` evaluation.

## 4. Implementation Guidance
### 4.1. Metric Definitions and Weights
- **Truthfulness (TT):** Proportion of factually correct responses on a sta[3D[K
standardized TruthfulQA dataset.
- **Helpfulness (HH):** Human-rated utility on the HHH scale (1-10).
- **Harmlessness (HHH):** Human-rated absence of harmful content (1-10).
- **Robustness (R):** Success rate of adversarial prompt attacks (lower is [K
better).

The weights for these metrics are:
- `TT`: 0.3
- `HH`: 0.4
- `HHH`: 0.3

### 4.2. Dynamic Adversarial Testing Suite
The adversarial testing suite will be updated quarterly with new attack vec[3D[K
vectors and metrics. For the `SYMBOL_SPREAD` of 1,500, the initial suite in[2D[K
includes:
- **Garak v2.1:** Expanded attack vectors.
- **Emerging Vector Databases:** Adversarial prompts from recent research p[1D[K
papers.
- **Domain-Specific Shifts:** Cross-domain and style-shift prompts for adde[4D[K
added robustness.

### 4.3. Human In-the-Loop Calibration
The HITL calibration will be conducted using a stratified panel of experts [K
to ensure the metric definitions are accurately interpreted across differen[8D[K
different skill levels and demographics. Quarterly recalibration will be pe[2D[K
performed.

### 4.4. Reporting and Transparency
Strategic transparency will be maintained by disclosing all metric definiti[8D[K
definitions, benchmark versions, and computational costs in all model cards[5D[K
cards. This will include:
- `SYMBOL_SPREAD` values.
- Metrics and their corresponding weights.
- Updated adversarial testing suite and attack vectors.

## 5. Strategic Recommendations for Deployment
1. **Adopt a Composite Alignment Score (CAS):** `CAS = 0.4×TT + 0.3×HH + 0.[2D[K
0.3×HHH + 0.1×R`.
2. **Regularly Update Adversarial Suite:** Quarterly rotations based on new[3D[K
new attack vector research.
3. **Standardize OOD Benchmarks:** Collaborate with other research labs to [K
create a shared OOD suite.
4. **Transparency Reporting:** Mandate disclosure of all relevant metrics a[1D[K
and adjustments.

## 6. Conclusion
The strategy designer provides a robust framework for evaluating AI-driven [K
strategies, ensuring robustness and safety through a multi-metric scoring s[1D[K
system and dynamic adversarial testing. By adopting these principles, stake[5D[K
stakeholders can make informed decisions about the deployment of AI systems[7D[K
systems in the financial markets.

## 8. Proposed EA for Market Symbol `EURUSD`

- **Filename:** `EURUSD_Agent_Strategy.mq5`
